# Appendix C: Every emoji QUILL can insert

This is the full list behind **Insert > Insert Emoji...** (`Alt+.`): all
3,781 standard emoji, grouped by category the same way the picker groups
them. Each row gives the emoji, its official name and a short description
of what it looks like. You never need this list to use the picker, which
searches by name, keyword or typed alias. It is here for when you would
like to browse at your own pace, or search the guide for a word.
